## `datepick.html` — בחירת תאריך

שלוש אופציות; **ב' לתאריך שנקבע עבור יובל** · ג' (בהצעות מחושבות, לא 'בעוד שבוע') + לוח שעות לזמן שהוא בוחר · א' נפסלה

```html
<!DOCTYPE html><html lang="he" dir="rtl"><head><meta charset="utf-8"><link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Assistant:wght@400;600;700&display=swap" rel="stylesheet"><style>
*{box-sizing:border-box;margin:0;padding:0;-webkit-font-smoothing:antialiased}
:root{--deep:#0A7A36;--deep2:#0E8C40;--live:#16A64B;--mint:#F0F8F3;--label:#0B1410;
--label2:rgba(45,60,52,.62);--label3:rgba(45,60,52,.34);--sep:rgba(45,60,52,.14);--amber:#96650F;--amberbg:#FCF3E2}
body{font-family:'Assistant',system-ui,sans-serif;background:#D9DCE1;width:1420px;display:flex;gap:20px;padding:26px 20px;align-items:flex-start}
.wrap{width:440px}
.vcap{padding:0 4px 11px;font-size:14px;color:#3a3a40;line-height:1.45;height:62px}
.vcap b{display:block;font-size:19px;font-weight:700;color:#141416}
.phone{width:440px;height:956px;border-radius:44px;overflow:hidden;position:relative;box-shadow:0 12px 30px rgba(0,0,0,.16)}
.screen{width:440px;height:956px;display:flex;flex-direction:column;position:relative;background:linear-gradient(180deg,#EFF2F4 0%,#E4E9EB 100%)}
.screen::before{content:"";position:absolute;top:0;left:0;right:0;height:470px;z-index:0;pointer-events:none;
 background:radial-gradient(125% 62% at 50% 0%, rgba(14,140,64,.20), rgba(14,140,64,.06) 55%, rgba(14,140,64,0) 82%)}
.screen::after{content:"";position:absolute;inset:0;z-index:1;pointer-events:none;opacity:.5;mix-blend-mode:multiply;
 background-image:url("data:image/svg+xml;utf8,<svg xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg' width='160' height='160'><filter id='n'><feTurbulence type='fractalNoise' baseFrequency='0.85' numOctaves='3'/></filter><rect width='160' height='160' filter='url(%23n)' opacity='0.42'/></svg>")}
.hero{position:relative;z-index:2;color:#fff;padding-bottom:44px;background:linear-gradient(168deg,var(--deep2) 0%,var(--deep) 66%,#096B2F 100%);
 border-radius:0 0 34px 34px;box-shadow:0 10px 26px rgba(6,60,28,.30),0 2px 6px rgba(6,60,28,.18)}
.hero::before{content:"";position:absolute;inset:0;border-radius:inherit;background:radial-gradient(85% 60% at 88% -8%, rgba(255,255,255,.26), rgba(255,255,255,0) 62%)}
.hero>*{position:relative;z-index:1}
.status{height:52px;display:flex;align-items:flex-end;justify-content:space-between;padding:0 30px 5px;font-size:15px;font-weight:600}
.navtop{padding:0 24px;height:26px;display:flex;align-items:center}
.appmark{width:22px;height:22px;opacity:.9}
.large{font-size:33px;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:-.9px;line-height:1.08;padding:7px 24px 0}
.navsub{font-size:14.5px;opacity:.72;padding:2px 24px 0}
.body{position:relative;z-index:3;flex:1;min-height:0;display:flex;flex-direction:column}
.pinwrap{position:relative;z-index:50;padding:0 14px;margin-top:-30px}
.stack{display:flex;flex-direction:column}
.card1{position:relative;z-index:3;background:#fff;border-radius:24px;overflow:hidden;box-shadow:0 10px 24px rgba(8,52,30,.16),0 2px 6px rgba(8,52,30,.08)}
.chead{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;padding:13px 18px;background:var(--amberbg)}
.dot{width:8px;height:8px;border-radius:50%;background:var(--amber);flex:none}
.clbl{font-size:13px;font-weight:700;color:var(--amber)}
.cnt{margin-inline-start:auto;font-size:12px;font-weight:700;color:#fff;background:var(--amber);border-radius:100px;padding:2px 10px}
.ctxt{font-size:17px;line-height:1.42;color:var(--label);padding:17px 18px 0}
.ctxt b{font-weight:700}
.btns{display:flex;gap:9px;padding:15px 18px 17px}
.cap{flex:1;text-align:center;padding:12px 0;border-radius:100px;font-size:16px;font-weight:700}
.yes{background:var(--deep);color:#fff;box-shadow:0 4px 10px rgba(10,122,54,.28)}
.no{background:#EFF1F3;color:var(--label2);font-weight:600}
.peek{background:#fff;border-radius:24px;height:26px;position:relative}
.p2{margin:-16px 12px 0;z-index:2;box-shadow:0 7px 14px rgba(8,52,30,.10)}
.more{text-align:center;font-size:13.5px;font-weight:700;color:var(--deep);padding:15px 0 0}
.feed{flex:1;overflow:hidden;padding:0 16px 10px;display:flex;flex-direction:column;justify-content:flex-end;gap:15px}
.card2{background:#fff;border-radius:22px;overflow:hidden;box-shadow:0 4px 12px rgba(8,52,30,.09)}
.h2{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;padding:12px 18px;background:var(--mint)}
.h2 .lbl{font-size:13px;font-weight:700;color:var(--deep)}
.h2 .t{margin-inline-start:auto;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:600;color:rgba(10,122,54,.55)}
.row{display:flex;align-items:baseline;gap:12px;padding:13px 18px;position:relative}
.row+.row::before{content:"";position:absolute;top:0;right:18px;left:18px;height:1px;background:var(--sep)}
.rt{font-size:15px;font-weight:700;color:var(--deep);min-width:46px;font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums}
.rb{font-size:17px;color:var(--label);line-height:1.3}
.rb small{display:block;font-size:13px;color:var(--label2);margin-top:2px}
.sub{font-size:12.5px;font-weight:700;color:var(--label2);letter-spacing:.3px;padding:15px 18px 3px}
.sub.sep{border-top:1px solid var(--sep);margin-top:6px;padding-top:15px}
.sub+.row::before{display:none}
.qtxt{font-size:17px;line-height:1.42;color:var(--label);padding:6px 18px 0}
.qtxt b{font-weight:700}
.btns.in{padding:13px 18px 17px}
.inputbar{position:relative;z-index:3;padding:10px 16px 26px;background:rgba(237,241,243,.9)}
.field{background:#fff;border-radius:100px;padding:7px 18px 7px 7px;font-size:17px;color:var(--label3);box-shadow:0 2px 7px rgba(8,52,30,.07);display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px}
.field span{flex:1}
.send{width:32px;height:32px;border-radius:50%;background:var(--deep);display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center}
.home{position:absolute;bottom:8px;left:50%;transform:translateX(-50%);width:140px;height:5px;border-radius:3px;background:#0005}
.tag{display:inline-block;font-size:12px;font-weight:700;border-radius:6px;padding:1px 7px;margin-inline-start:6px}

.phone{height:956px}
.hero{padding-bottom:22px}.hero.big{padding-bottom:44px}
.back{font-size:15px;font-weight:600;opacity:.85;padding:2px 22px 0}
.large{font-size:30px}
.pg{position:relative;z-index:3;padding:14px 12px 0;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:12px;flex:1;min-height:0;overflow:hidden}
.seg{display:flex;background:rgba(45,60,52,.08);border-radius:12px;padding:3px}
.seg div{flex:1;text-align:center;font-size:14px;font-weight:600;color:var(--label2);padding:7px 0;border-radius:9px}
.seg .on{background:#fff;color:var(--label);box-shadow:0 1px 4px rgba(0,0,0,.1)}
.box{background:#fff;border-radius:20px;box-shadow:0 4px 12px rgba(8,52,30,.09);overflow:hidden}
.wk{display:grid;grid-template-columns:34px repeat(7,1fr);position:relative}
.wk .hd{font-size:11.5px;font-weight:700;color:var(--label2);text-align:center;padding:8px 0 6px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--sep)}
.wk .hd b{display:block;font-size:15px;color:var(--label)}
.tc{position:relative}
.hl{position:absolute;left:0;right:0;border-top:1px solid var(--sep)}
.tl{position:absolute;right:2px;font-size:10px;color:var(--label3);transform:translateY(-6px)}
.ev{position:absolute;left:2px;right:2px;border-radius:6px;font-size:10px;font-weight:700;color:#fff;padding:2px 3px;overflow:hidden;line-height:1.15}
.mg{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(7,1fr)}
.mg .dw{font-size:12px;font-weight:700;color:var(--label2);text-align:center;padding:8px 0}
.mc{height:74px;border-top:1px solid var(--sep);padding:4px 2px;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:2px;align-items:stretch}
.mc .n{font-size:13px;text-align:center;color:var(--label);font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums}
.mc .n.t{color:#fff;background:var(--deep);border-radius:10px;width:22px;margin:0 auto;font-weight:700}
.mc .n.sel{outline:2px solid var(--deep);border-radius:10px;width:22px;margin:0 auto;font-weight:700}
.bar{height:5px;border-radius:3px}
.chip{font-size:9.5px;font-weight:700;border-radius:5px;padding:1px 2px;text-align:center;white-space:nowrap;overflow:hidden}
.li{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;padding:11px 16px;position:relative;font-size:15.5px;color:var(--label)}
.li+.li::before{content:"";position:absolute;top:0;right:16px;left:16px;height:1px;background:var(--sep)}
.li .d{width:9px;height:9px;border-radius:50%;flex:none}
.li .tm{font-size:14px;font-weight:700;color:var(--deep);min-width:86px;font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums;direction:ltr;text-align:right}
.li .k{font-size:12.5px;color:var(--label2)}
.gbtn{background:#fff;border-radius:16px;padding:14px;text-align:center;font-weight:700;color:var(--deep);font-size:16px;box-shadow:0 3px 10px rgba(8,52,30,.08)}
.strip{display:flex;gap:6px;padding:0 14px;margin-top:-22px;position:relative;z-index:40}
.sd{flex:1;background:#fff;border-radius:16px;padding:8px 0 9px;text-align:center;box-shadow:0 4px 12px rgba(8,52,30,.12)}
.sd .w{font-size:11.5px;color:var(--label2);font-weight:700}.sd .n{font-size:18px;font-weight:700;color:var(--label)}
.sd .dots{display:flex;gap:3px;justify-content:center;height:6px;margin-top:3px}.sd .dots i{width:5px;height:5px;border-radius:50%}
.sd.on{background:var(--deep)}.sd.on .w,.sd.on .n{color:#fff}
.feed{flex:1;overflow:hidden;padding:0 14px 10px;display:flex;flex-direction:column;justify-content:flex-end;gap:13px}
.me{align-self:flex-end;max-width:76%;background:linear-gradient(165deg,#1AB455,var(--live));color:#fff;border-radius:22px;padding:11px 17px;font-size:17px;box-shadow:0 4px 12px rgba(22,166,75,.26)}
.yr{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(3,1fr);gap:12px 10px;padding:12px}
.ym b{display:block;font-size:12.5px;color:var(--label);margin-bottom:4px}
.ymg{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(7,1fr);gap:2px}
.ymg i{aspect-ratio:1;border-radius:2px;background:#EEF1F2}
.ymg i.e{background:none}
.ymg i.u{background:repeating-linear-gradient(45deg,#E3E7E9 0 2px,#F4F6F7 2px 4px)}
.leg{display:flex;gap:12px;flex-wrap:wrap;font-size:12px;color:var(--label2);padding:0 14px 12px}
.leg span{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:5px}.leg i{width:11px;height:11px;border-radius:3px;display:inline-block}
.ah{position:sticky;top:0;font-size:13px;font-weight:700;color:var(--label2);padding:10px 16px 6px;background:#F7F9F9}
.dayh{font-size:13px;font-weight:700;color:var(--label);padding:10px 16px 2px}
.dayh span{color:var(--label2);font-weight:400}
.hint{text-align:center;font-size:12.5px;color:var(--label2);display:flex;justify-content:center;gap:6px;align-items:center}
```
